I don't know how long I continue like this but the next time I start to bear down Darvik pops
his head up from his position between my parted legs. The silvery lines of his scars appear
stark against his copper-shaded features. His earlier frown is gone, now replaced with a
flaring smile of relief.

"He is coming! I see the head!"

Thank god!

Adrenaline surges and I squeeze as hard as I can. A slight tearing sensation follows but most
of its sting is numbed by more of that delicious gas forcing its way through my nose. Jerking,
I reach out. For someone. For anyone. A warm palm slips against mine, our fingers
entangling.

"You are doing very well, my Lartee."

I find Memnis, peering at his face which seems to have lightened a shade or two. I see a
fierce determination in his eyes, and also fear. He's afraid for me.

"Scream louder if you must."

And that's exactly what I do when the next round strikes. I howl like a woman possessed, the
sound purely animal. My throat becomes raw yet the action of it provides some relief too. I
listen to each of my male's demands, following them to the letter and throwing my full trust
and well-being into their care. Push. Rest. Breathe. Calm. Repeat. Over and over. And never
once do they leave my side. I'm so incredibly grateful to them that I start to sob mid-push and
a tear slips down my cheek. It is wiped away promptly, along with my sweat which seems to
be pouring from my skin like a running faucet. My back is drenched. The bench beneath me
is slippery and sticks to my skin unpleasantly.

The baby moves, jolting me back to awareness. With another shriek I almost double over,
squeezing at the hand in mine as I force all my strength into my next efforts. My uterus
spasms wildly. My vagina feels like it's being torn open yet I feel the baby move through it
some more. And then…

There is nothing. The pressure, the fullness…

It's gone.

I rear back to the bench, panting like crazy.

A mere terrifying second of silence passes before a sound comes, splitting through it like the
caw of a wild bird.

A cry.

The shrill cry of a baby.



"Oh," I choke, realising what's happening.

Through the chaos, I weakly manage to lift my head and find Sin'Chila holding a tiny little
bundle of flesh to his broad chest.

Pale grey skin.

I see a set of healthy-looking legs wriggle as the cries increase tenfold, urgent and needy for
its mother. For me.

"Give him to me," I rasp, already holding out my arms, needing to feel him. "Please."

"Soon, chakria. Be patient a little while longer."

Sin'Chila briefly carries the child to the other side of the room to a nearby station. Yaknaur
and Darvik join at his side and they start to fiddle with various tools and scanners there. Their
backs are facing towards me and a shard of concern rakes.

Was he Ok? Was he hurt?

One of them swipes at a screen and it flares up into life, floating directly in front of them.

"All is well."

They all give a rumble in relief and I join them with a huge sigh.

Carefully, Sin'Chila turns and returns to my side. I catch the lingering stare he gives to the
child, noticing the pride and awe there before he slowly lowers our son into my eager
embrace.

As soon as I look down at his beautiful tiny face I crumple with sobs. All the stress and all
the emotions I'd been fighting back come crashing down around me and I am washed away
under its torrent.

"He's so... perfect," I say between a hiccup, sniffling loudly.

I feel the bond between us instantly blaze into life. Longingly, my eyes scan over him,
checking him closely. I take in the light charcoal coloured skin that seems smooth and
flawless. His Yautja facial structure is familiar, just like his fathers', though hairless and
tuskless. His tiny mouth spreads wide in a fleshy yawn and his eyes crack open, meeting
mine. I am overwhelmed all over again to find that they are dark brown and human-like. Like
mine.

"I can't believe you're finally here," I coo, pressing a delicate kiss to his forehead.

Memnis chirps, leaning it too to gaze at him with open wonder.

"We have been waiting for you, little Tizo." He flashes me a grin and I reach up, stroking at
his angular cheek.



Sin'Chila and Yaknaur join us, each of them taking their turn to touch the baby and welcome
him into our ever-expanding family.

Darvik hovers near the opposite end of the table. Hesitant. I hold out a hand, encouraging
him to come forward as well. He shifts on his feet, somewhat awkward and I can all but hear
the thoughts that must be going through his mind right now as he watches us. The child was
not his. He thinks he's intruding. That he has no right to be here even though he'd pretty much
been both 'midwife' and 'doctor' throughout the whole thing.

I frown, only to laugh a little when Yaknaur strides over to him and yanks him forward.

"Come! Do not hide now, Vindas!" Yaknaur slaps the other male on the back in a hearty
gesture of peace between them. "Now is not the time to be a coward."

Darvik closes the distance and smiles at me, which I return with warmth. He sweeps a lock of
dampened hair away from my temple before peering down at our baby. The big giant trills at
the youngling, the tip of his tail sweeping up to dangle before his face. Tizo's chubby little
clawless fingers clamp and hold onto it.

"A miracle…" Darvik whispers, unable to look away.

"Let me hold him." Yaknaur steps in, holding his arms open to me with a greedy gleam in his
eyes.

Memnis grumbles with disapproval. "Do not hog him all to yourself, brother. We all want a
turn as well."

The two males growl at each other, playful, but low with threat. Possessive.

"I am the second eldest. It is my right..."

"Come on, guys," I pipe up with a tired laugh. "Take it easy. You can all cuddle him. Just be
gentle, Ok? Be sure to support his head." With that, I hand Tizo over to Yaknaur who all but
sings his joy and gathers him to his breast. It was a strange thing to see; a massive alien
warrior carrying and snuggling a tiny baby who could only just fit into the palms of both his
huge hands. I see his pride and could already tell these guys were going to be such loving and
doting fathers.

I battle against a yawn suddenly. Out of nowhere, exhaustion hits, barrelling into me with all
the power of a train at full speed. There isn't a single part of my body that isn't aching or sore
and yet my joy is purely euphoric.

I lean back to the bench, shivering as the sweat on my skin turns cool. Seeing my trembles,
Sin'Chila leaves my side, only to return with a blanket. As soon as he slides the thickened
hide over me I am almost instantly warm again, like magic.

All eyes slide away from Tizo and land on me.

"Rest, chakria. Sleep." Sin'Chila trills. "We will take care of our little warrior until you
awaken. You have done well… Sleep."



I preen at his praise, my chest flooding with love for them all. Still smiling, I allow myself to
drift, fully confident that they would watch over me.

My eyes shutter close. And then… I sleep.

 

 

Yaknaur's POV

 

The young one in his hold is unlike anything he has ever beheld. Reverent, he scrutinises the
tiny creature, his breast aching as his kava opens his eyes and stares up at him. He is
everything Yaknaur imagined him to be and more besides. His little body is light, barely even
felt by his hands. So fragile. So vulnerable.

As one, the rest of his clan brothers press closer, forming a circle around the babe as they all
take him in.

Yaknaur's throat clogs and he finally lifts his focus to his eldest kin, Sin'Chila. None of them
utters a word. None of them speaks. There were none to give for this precious moment they
shared. This was their son. Their kava. One born from the womb of their hooman mate.
Nothing could begin to describe how fortunate he felt.

He glances sideways to his female who lays resting nearby, quiet and still. Her fiery mane
shines in the light, darkened by the toils of her labour. Her eyes are closed, flickering in sleep
as the smooth paleness of her skin gleams. His mind is stolen by the image of her, his body
overtaken with the driving need to protect. To provide. To possess.

For so long he had imagined this. Of creating new life and continuing the bloodline of his
people. And here it was. The proof of all his strife and endless searching contained within
this very room. Truly magnificent.

An urge grips him and he turns to face Darvik. The larger male watches him, tail curling idly
behind him, his expression keen and fixed on the child. Silent, he holds little Tizo out to him
in offering.

"Here... Take him."

Darvik blinks, taken aback. "Me?"

Yaknaur huffs a chuckle. "Who else? This is my son. Our son. We are one clan now, and so,
you will become a father to him also," he says firmly, brooking no refusal.

Reluctant, Darvik gathers the tiny male to him, inhaling his scent and crooning a low purr as
the youngling begins to stir.



He bows his head in a gesture of respect to Yaknaur and the others. "I am truly pleased that
you would honour me with such a gift. I will treasure him. Always."

Yaknaur nods back, flashing a small, smug grin. "Good!"

The oath was made; a pledge between them sealed for the prosperity of their clan and the
safety of their newborn. No one would ever take the young one from their keeping or ever
harm their female. They would defend and protect them until their last remaining breaths in
this life.

With his Yautja blood surging through his veins, Yaknaur growls, unable to bite the noise
back. Without warning, the bundle in Darvik's hold begins to wriggle harder, a shrill cry
coming from his little mouth.

They all wince and peer down at Tizo and then slowly rotate towards Yaknaur with a scowl.

"Must you be so noisy?"

Yaknaur merely shrugs a shoulder, sheepish. They all return to the howling child, perplexed
and anxiously looking towards each other for direction.

"Now what do we do?" Memnis finally asks.

No one answers. No one knows.

"Perhaps he is hungry?" Sin'Chila suggests, even though it lacks some of his usual all-
knowing wisdom.

"Yes. Good idea."

They all straighten and then go, as one, in search of something suitable to feed their new
charge.



Chapter 55

Epilogue

 

I stare around at the room of treasures around me. This chamber… I remember it well. With
its plain steel walls and bleak interior, the space would have been empty if not for the
mountain of items piled in its centre. From books to clothes, to weird and wonderful statues.
Broken devices, phones. Jewellery. All of them familiar and taken from fellow humans at
someplace in time or another. Sin'Chila had shown me this place long ago and, unlike back
then, I no longer felt the pang of missing my homeland, Earth.

I'd been away from my world for so long now that I was struggling to recall what some of it
looked like. Despite that fact, I didn't miss any of it. Its bustling cities were nothing but a
faded memory to me now, along with its people and society.

This new life I had was a wondrous thing, I thought to myself with a deepening sigh. No
more paying taxes. No more striving to fit in with the rest of the world. I belonged here;
amidst a wild jungle that was lethal and dangerous at every turn. It was a raw place, reliant on
sheer survival. But it was also where my heart longed to be most of all. A place where my
hunters were. Where I belonged. With them.

My eyes skim around the items further, contemplative.

"Does your heartache to return, Chakria?" Sin'Chila says from behind me suddenly, making
me jump a little. How long had he been there? I hadn't even heard him approach. He presses
his larger shape against my back before adding, "We took you from all that you knew, from
your people. I would not blame you if--"

I whirl and stop his words with a hand to his mouth. My fingers skim his tusks gently.

"No," I say, shaking my head vehemently. "Please, don't ever think that. Maybe, a long time
ago I might have wanted to go back to Earth... but now?… No. This is my home now. You are
my home, Sin'Chila."

His jaws flutter into a slight grimacing smile. "You are certain?"

I stroke his cheek and then lean up onto my tiptoes to kiss it lovingly.

"I've never been more sure. What more could I ask for?"

They had all taught me so much about myself. Of whom I was and wanted to be. I was no
longer that timid young woman who never spoke up or demanded things for herself. I was
someone better. Stronger. Wiser. When I'd first come to this planet, I had been fragile and
lonely; abandoned by Jason who had been my entire world at the time. But then these Yautja
hunters had come along. Hulking males who should have killed me, yet instead had chosen



mercy and kept me as their own, loving me more than any 'civilized' human had ever done in
my entire life.

It wasn't exactly like the romance stories you read about in books. Oh no. It was something
infinitely more meaningful and precious. I had never felt more complete. My existence had
never felt more right and it was all due to them.

My males. My mates.

I scrutinize Sin'Chila's alien expression closely, searching it for any further sign of doubt,
finding none. I relax and then smile at him. I begin to speak, but a sound echoes, jerking us to
attention. The shriek of a baby crying has me turning towards the door with a frown.

"Wow, that kid's got some lungs on him," I joke lightly. "Clearly he takes after Yaknaur."

Sin'Chila grunts a laugh. "Your kava calls for you, chakria. He seems to be giving Memnis
much grief today."

"Ah, the joys of being a new father."

All of us were in the same boat, still trying to learn how to be good parents to our ever-
growing son. It was a work in progress, to say the least. One thing was for certain though. My
little man needed and wanted for nothing; my hunters made sure of that.

The wailing continues and we both share a look and wince.

"Perhaps I should go and rescue Memnis."

"Indeed. Would be wise."

Sin'Chila bends forward, rubbing his mandibles into my hair with affection before letting me
step away from him. I barely make it a few paces towards the door when it slides open with a
woosh.

Suddenly, Memnis is there, holding aloft a wriggling and screaming Tizo. The youngling's
face is wrinkled up and darkened with his displeasure, whilst an expression of sheer panic
shines across my mates face in an almost comical way.

"He will not stop," Memnis chirps fast with blatant worry. "He is very needy. What should I
do?"

I lift a brow and then reach up, plucking the baby from him. I cuddle him to me and as soon
as Tizo feels me against him, he instantly stills and quietens. Memnis trills in relief and his
shoulders slump.

I bounce Tizo lightly against me and make my way towards the doorway again, carrying him
with me. "Come on, you little monster. Let's get your belly all filled up and then get you to
sleep. It's past your bedtime."



I cross the threshold and enter into the narrow metal corridor beyond. Footsteps clang and I
glance up as Yaknaur and Darvik head up the ramp and enter into the ship. They're each
carrying the body of an animal of some kind over their shoulders; a sure sign that their
hunting this morning had been successful. They shoot me a grin each, clearly pleased with
themselves.

"Come, my female," Yaknaur boasts. "I have gathered many of the Ziluni fruits that you like
most. You can have them all. Let us feast!"

Sin'Chila and Memnis exit out of the door behind me, moving to trail after the others towards
the deck.

"At last, finally..." Sin'Chila grunts with a teasing gleam in his eyes. "I was beginning to fear
you had become lost or been eaten by some wild beast. What took you so long?"

Yaknaur snarls but then ignores the jibe. And I had no doubt that, if he knew what the gesture
meant, he'd probably flash his brother the middle finger.

My legs move to follow them. But then, I pause, peering back towards the room with all the
human possessions strewn about inside. It reminded me of who I had been and where I had
once come from. Solemn, I linger as a deep sense of something crawls through me…

The feeling of saying farewell.

Maybe not forever, but to the life that I had once known and would never return to again. No
regrets. No sadness. Just a calm sense of acceptance.

"Chakria?" Sin'Chila calls and I look to find he'd stopped to wait for me.

"I'm coming," I call back and start to head towards him with a loving smile.

The door, sensing my departure, seals shut behind me, closing off that old life I once knew.

And like that, I make my way onto the deck to join them all. And it is there that I stay, with
my boy clasped in my arms and my four devoted males at my side. I was at peace. For they
were my life, my future and purpose combined. My sense of belonging. My home and my
family.

Always and forever.






The END
